`````python
                    references+=disposition.get('evidence_refs',[])
                    if disposition.get('amount_ref'):references.append(disposition['amount_ref'])
                    references+=disposition.get('component_refs',[])
                    if disposition.get('total_leave_ref'):references.append(disposition['total_leave_ref'])
            used = {self.r['tables'][ref['table']]['source_id'] for ref in references if ref.get('table') in self.r.get('tables', {})}
            used |= display_confirmation_sources
            if role=='payroll_confirmed':used|=set(self.r.get('payroll_original_source_ids',[]))
            raw_names = ('raw_attendance', 'raw_rewards') if role == 'attendance_confirmed' else ('raw_attendance', 'raw_rewards', 'raw_insurance')
            used |= {self.r['tables'][self.r[x]['table']]['source_id'] for x in raw_names if self.r.get(x, {}).get('table') in self.r.get('tables', {})}
            scope_table = self.r.get('tables', {}).get(self.r.get('scope_table'), {})
            if scope_table.get('source_id'): used.add(scope_table['source_id'])
            original = self.r.get('raw_attendance', {}).get('original_table')
            if original in self.r.get('tables', {}): used.add(self.r['tables'][original]['source_id'])
            for stage in before:
                used |= {value for key, value in self.r.get('template_bindings', {}).get(stage, {}).items() if key.endswith('_source_id') and isinstance(value, str) and value in self.sources}
            used.discard(c['source_id'])
            if not used <= set(c.get('dependencies', {})): self.error('确认快照遗漏实际前置来源:' + c['source_id'])

    def template_receipts(self):
        linked = {}
        required_stages = self.required_delivery_stages()
        for stage in getattr(self, 'stages', ()):
            if stage not in required_stages: continue
            stage_spec = self.r.get('stages', {}).get(stage, {})
            table = self.tables.get(stage_spec.get('table'))
            binding = self.r.get('template_bindings', {}).get(stage)
            if not binding:
                self.tech_wait(stage + ':缺指定正式模板的实际复核绑定；省略template_required不能豁免')
                linked[stage] = {'verified': False, 'reason': '缺实际模板绑定'}; continue
            if not table:
                self.tech_wait(stage + ':尚无正式模板实际产出，保留独立明细'); continue
            try:
                actual = self.sources[table['spec']['source_id']]
                def pinned(source_field, digest_field):
                    source = self.sources.get(binding.get(source_field))
                    if source is None: raise MissingEvidence('缺有效来源:' + source_field)
                    if binding.get(digest_field) != source['sha256']: raise ValueError('固定SHA与实际来源不符:' + source_field)
                    return source
                if binding.get('kind', 'xlsx') == 'xlsx':
                    if table['spec']['format'] != 'xlsx': raise MissingEvidence('当前仅有计算辅助表，尚无实际正式xlsx模板成品')
                    registry = pinned('registry_source_id', 'registry_sha256')
                    plan = pinned('plan_source_id', 'plan_sha256')
                    receipt = pinned('receipt_source_id', 'receipt_sha256')
                    kwargs = {}
                    if binding.get('recalculation_log_source_id'):
                        log = pinned('recalculation_log_source_id', 'recalculation_log_sha256')
                        kwargs.update(recalculation_log=log['resolved'], recalculation_log_sha256=log['sha256'])
                    guard = _module('payroll-operations', 'template_guard.py')
                    checked = guard.verify(registry['resolved'], registry['sha256'], plan['resolved'], actual['resolved'], receipt['resolved'], None, **kwargs)
                elif binding.get('kind') == 'legacy_xls':
                    if stage != 'tax' or table['spec']['format'] != 'xls': raise ValueError('legacy_xls仅用于实际政府税表原始xls')
                    template = pinned('template_source_id', 'template_sha256')
                    policy_source = pinned('policy_source_id', 'policy_sha256')
                    policy = json.loads(policy_source['resolved'].read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
                    guard = _module('payroll-operations', 'legacy_template_guard.py')
                    checked = guard.inspect(template['resolved'], template['sha256'], None, actual['resolved'], clear_original_yellow=policy.get('clear_original_yellow') is True, clear_all_fills=policy.get('clear_all_fills') is True)
                else: raise ValueError('未知模板复核路径')
                linked[stage] = {'structure': checked, 'actual_output_sha256': actual['sha256']}
                if not checked.get('passed'):
                    for e in checked.get('errors', ['未通过']): self.tech_error(stage + ':实际模板重放拒绝:' + str(e))
                    continue
                if checked.get('finalized_output_sha256') != actual['sha256']: raise ValueError('实际模板复核未绑定本次最终SHA')
                if stage == 'payroll':
                    registration = json.loads(registry['resolved'].read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
                    policy = registration.get('policy', {})
                    originals = set(self.r.get('payroll_original_source_ids', [])) | {key for key, value in self.sources.items() if value.get('role') in ('payroll_original', 'wage_original')}
                    if policy.get('require_source_comments') is not True or not originals:
                        self.tech_wait('payroll:缺本次工资原件批注强制保护或实际原件清单')
                    registered = set()
                    for item in policy.get('source_comments', []):
                        value = item.get('source', {}); path = Path(str(value.get('path', '')))
                        if not path.is_absolute(): path = registry['resolved'].parent / path
                        registered.add((str(path.resolve()), value.get('sha256')))
                    for original_id in originals:
                        value = self.sources.get(original_id)
                        if not value or (str(value['resolved'].resolve()), value['sha256']) not in registered or original_id == table['spec']['source_id']:
                            self.tech_wait('payroll:工资原件未完整登记批注盘点（0条也须登记）:' + str(original_id))
                    if not registration.get('comment_source_inventories') or 'source_comment_inventory' not in checked:
                        self.tech_wait('payroll:当前模板能力或回执缺实际原件批注盘点，不能自称保真')
                    formula_plan = pinned('formula_audit_plan_source_id', 'formula_audit_plan_sha256')
                    plan_data = json.loads(formula_plan['resolved'].read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
                    plan_data['source_view_inventory'] = checked.get('source_view_inventory')
                    plan_data['attendance_identity_column'] = table['spec']['columns'].get('name',plan_data.get('attendance_identity_column'))
                    if plan_data.get('sheet') != table['spec'].get('sheet') or plan_data.get('first_row') != table['spec']['start_row'] or plan_data.get('last_row') != table['spec']['end_row']: raise ValueError('公式审计计划未覆盖实际工资人员范围')
                    for exemption in plan_data.get('pending_formula_exemptions', {}).values(): self.refs(exemption.get('source_refs'), '工资公式未核口径来源')
                    checked_formulas = _module('payroll-operations', 'audit_payroll_workbook.py').audit(actual['resolved'], plan_data)
                    linked[stage]['formula_audit'] = checked_formulas
                    for e in checked_formulas.get('errors', []): self.tech_error(stage + ':实际公式/缓存拒绝:' + str(e))
                    for e in checked_formulas.get('technical_pending', []): self.tech_wait(stage + ':公式技术未完成:' + str(e))
                    if checked_formulas.get('pending_totals') or checked_formulas.get('pending_rows'): self.wait(stage + ':实际公式/合计仍有待核事项')
                native = binding.get('native_recalculation', {})
                if native.get('run') is not True:
                    self.tech_wait(stage + ':未执行隔离副本的实际原生重算与缓存核对；字符串passed无效'); continue
                checks = native.get('linkage_checks', [])
                if stage == 'payroll' and not checks:
                    self.tech_wait(stage + ':缺实际可编辑事假输入到请假扣款/应发/实发的联动核对'); continue
                if stage == 'payroll':
                    from openpyxl.utils.cell import coordinate_to_tuple
                    source_links=(checked.get('source_view_inventory') or {}).get('links',[])
                    if not any(c.get('kind')=='attendance_snapshot' for c in checks): self.tech_wait(stage + ':缺工资内考勤快照业务金额到T的实际联动核对')
                    if not any(c.get('kind')!='attendance_snapshot' for c in checks): self.tech_wait(stage + ':缺原有请假输入联动核对')
                    for check in checks:
                        if check.get('kind')=='attendance_snapshot':
                            links=[x for x in source_links if x['view_sheet']==check.get('sheet') and x['value_cell']==check.get('input_cell') and x['target_sheet']==table['spec']['sheet']]
                            if len(links)!=1: raise ValueError('考勤联动输入未绑定实际同人可编辑快照金额')
                            link=links[0]; target_row=coordinate_to_tuple(link['target_cell'])[0]
                            if target_row not in table['active']: raise ValueError('考勤联动目标不是本次人员行')
                            outputs=check.get('outputs',[])
                            if not any(x.get('item')=='attendance_deduction' and x.get('sheet')==link['target_sheet'] and x.get('cell')==link['target_cell'] for x in outputs): raise ValueError('考勤快照联动缺实际工资T输出')
                            for item in outputs:
                                if item.get('sheet')!=table['spec']['sheet'] or item.get('cell')!=str(table['spec']['columns'].get(item.get('item')))+str(target_row): raise ValueError('考勤跨页联动输出未绑定实际工资字段')
                            continue
                        if check.get('sheet') != table['spec']['sheet'] or coordinate_to_tuple(check['input_cell'])[0] not in table['active']: raise ValueError('联动测试未绑定实际工资人员行')
                        expected_items = {'leave_deduction', 'gross', 'net'}
                        if not expected_items <= {x.get('item') for x in check.get('outputs', [])}: raise ValueError('联动测试缺请假扣款/应发/实发')
                        for item in check['outputs']:
                            if item.get('item') in expected_items and item.get('cell') != str(table['spec']['columns'].get(item['item'])) + str(coordinate_to_tuple(check['input_cell'])[0]): raise ValueError('联动输出未绑定实际工资字段')
                native_checked = _module('payroll-operations', 'native_recalc_verify.py').verify_native_copy(actual['resolved'], actual['sha256'], checks)
                linked[stage]['native_recalculation'] = native_checked
                for e in native_checked.get('errors', []): self.tech_error(stage + ':原生复核:' + str(e))
                unresolved=native_checked.get('pending',[])
                data_only=bool(native_checked.get('actual_engine') and unresolved and not native_checked.get('errors') and all('联动输出仍为空白' in e or '缺核定数值预期' in e for e in unresolved))
                linked[stage]['native_linkage_complete']=native_checked.get('verified') is True
                linked[stage]['native_technical_verified']=native_checked.get('verified') is True or data_only
                if data_only:self.wait(stage + ':原生已执行，金额依赖/预期仍待核:' + ';'.join(unresolved))
                elif native_checked.get('verified') is not True:self.tech_wait(stage + ':原生重算/缓存未证实:' + ';'.join(unresolved))
            except MissingEvidence as exc: self.tech_wait(stage + ':模板实物复核资料不足:' + str(exc))
            except (ValueError, TypeError, OSError, KeyError) as exc: self.tech_error(stage + ':模板实物复核拒绝:' + str(exc))
        for stage,item in linked.items():
            item['technical_verified']=bool(item.get('structure',{}).get('passed') and item.get('native_technical_verified') and not any(x.startswith(stage+':') for x in self.technical_errors+self.technical_pending))
        self.evidence['template_receipts'] = linked

    def maternity_handoff(self):
        for person in self.people:
            fact = self.facts.get((person, 'payroll', 'maternity_leave'))
            if not fact or fact.get('status') == 'pending' or number(fact.get('amount')) in (None, 0): continue
            if number(fact.get('amount')) < 0 or fact.get('included_in') != 'other_deduction':
                self.error(person + ':产假月中差额须正数计入其他个人扣款，不能另入请假/考勤/入离职差额'); continue
            original_ref = fact.get('manual_original_ref')
            if not original_ref:
                self.wait(person + ':产假差额缺原其他个人扣款格定位，不能判断是否覆盖人工值'); continue
            try: original = self.ref(original_ref, self.people[person])
            except ValueError as exc: self.error(person + ':产假原人工值:' + str(exc)); continue
            bucket = self.facts.get((person, 'payroll', 'other_deduction'))
            if original not in (None, ''):
                approval_id = fact.get('merge_confirmation_source_id')
                approval = self.sources.get(approval_id, {})
                confirmation = next((c for c in self.r.get('confirmations', []) if c.get('source_id') == approval_id), {})
                ref = fact.get('merge_amount_ref')
                source_ids = {self.r['tables'][original_ref['table']]['source_id']} | {self.r['tables'][r['table']]['source_id'] for r in fact.get('source_refs', []) if r.get('table') in self.r.get('tables', {})}
                if approval_id not in getattr(self, 'confirmed_sources', set()) or approval.get('role') != 'manual_approval' or not source_ids <= set(confirmation.get('dependencies', {})) or not ref or self.r.get('tables', {}).get(ref.get('table'), {}).get('source_id') != approval_id:
                    self.wait(person + ':原其他个人扣款已有人工值，产假是否已包含及合并金额未实际确认'); continue
                try: approved_amount = self.ref(ref, self.people[person])
                except ValueError as exc: self.error(person + ':产假合并依据:' + str(exc)); continue
                if bucket and bucket.get('status') in DONE and not equal(bucket.get('amount'), approved_amount): self.error(person + ':产假合并后实际其他个人扣款与本人确认金额不符')
            elif bucket and bucket.get('status') in DONE:
                if not any(d.get('stage') == 'payroll' and d.get('item') == 'maternity_leave' for d in bucket.get('depends_on', [])):
                    self.wait(person + ':缺产假专项差额到实际其他个人扣款桶的组成核对')

    def comment_business_review(self):
        inventory=self.evidence.get('template_receipts',{}).get('payroll',{}).get('structure',{}).get('source_comment_inventory')
        if inventory is None:return
        def resolve(ref,key):
            value=self.ref(ref,self.people[key]);table=self.tables[ref['table']]
            return value,table['rows'][ref['row']],self.sources[table['spec']['source_id']]
        def components(table_id,key):
            table=self.tables[table_id]
            if not {'component_id','month','amount'}<=set(table['spec']['columns']):raise ValueError('完整组成表缺实际项目标识/月/金额映射')
            refs=[]
            for n,actual in table['rows'].items():
                if n<table['spec']['start_row'] or not self.matches(actual,self.people[key]):continue
                if not actual.get('month'):raise ValueError('本人组成明细有未定月份，不能宣称覆盖完整')
                if actual['month']==self.r['month']:refs.append({'table':table_id,'row':n,'column':'amount'})
            return refs
        rows=_module('payroll-operations','payroll_comment_review.py').review_comments(inventory,self.people,self.r['month'],self.r.get('comment_dispositions',[]),resolve,components)
        states=[];affected=set()
        for row in rows:
            key=row['employee_key']
            if row['effective_status']=='resolved':
                if row.get('adopted_total_leave') is not None:
                    fact=self.facts.get((key,'payroll','leave_deduction'))
                    if not fact or not equal(fact.get('amount'),row['adopted_total_leave']):self.error(str(key)+':本人已核完整请假扣款总额与实际S事实不符')
                continue
            self.wait(str(key)+':原工资事假清算批注业务待核:'+row['source_sheet']+'!'+row['source_cell']+':'+row['reason'])
            if key not in self.people:continue
            affected.add(key)
            targets=[('payroll',x) for x in ('leave_deduction','gross','finance','net','bank')]+[('tax','taxable'),('payslip','gross'),('payslip','net')]
            for stage,item in targets:
                fact=self.facts.get((key,stage,item))
                stage_spec=self.r.get('stages',{}).get(stage,{})
                table=self.tables.get(stage_spec.get('table'))
                own=[d for d in stage_spec.get('dispositions',[]) if d.get('employee_key')==key and d.get('row') is not None]
                refs=list((fact or {}).get('value_refs',[]))
                if own and table and item in table['spec']['columns']:
                    actual_ref={'table':stage_spec['table'],'row':own[0]['row'],'column':item}
                    if actual_ref not in refs:refs.append(actual_ref)
                if not fact and not refs:continue
                fact=fact or {'employee_key':key,'stage':stage,'item':item,'status':'pending','amount':None}
                values=[]
                if fact.get('status')!='pending' or fact.get('amount') is not None:self.error(key+':原清算批注未决但'+stage+'.'+item+'仍声明已算/不适用或金额')
                for ref in refs:
                    try:
                        value=self.ref(ref,self.people[key]);values.append({'ref':ref,'actual_value':value})
                        if number(value) is not None:self.tech_error('payroll:'+key+':原清算批注未决，实际'+stage+'.'+item+'仍显示数值；S及依赖应发须真实待核，不能仅改审计状态')
                    except ValueError as exc:self.error(key+':清算待核实物定位:'+str(exc))
                states.append({'employee_key':key,'stage':stage,'item':item,'effective_status':'pending','effective_amount':None,'declared_status':fact.get('status'),'declared_amount':fact.get('amount'),'actual_values':values,'reason':'原工资清算批注尚未逐人处置'})
                self.facts[(key,stage,item)]=dict(fact,status='pending',amount=None)
        self.evidence['comment_business_review']={'items':rows,'effective_facts':states,'note':'批注对象保留与业务处置分别核验；不修改源或成品，不推定清算小时或扣款'}
        for key in affected:self.check_payroll_totals(key)
        if any(x.startswith('payroll:') for x in self.technical_errors):self.evidence['template_receipts']['payroll']['technical_verified']=False

    def attendance_handoff_review(self):
        checked=_module('payroll-operations','attendance_fact_handoff.py').verify_handoff(self.r,self.base,self)
        self.evidence['attendance_fact_handoff']=checked
        for message in checked['errors']:self.tech_error('attendance:最终人资事实交接拒绝:'+message)
        for message in checked['pending']:self.tech_wait('attendance:最终人资事实交接未完成:'+message)
        for message in checked['data_pending']:self.wait(message)
        if not checked['passed'] and 'attendance' in self.evidence.get('template_receipts',{}):self.evidence['template_receipts']['attendance']['technical_verified']=False

    def run(self):
        if self.r.get('schema_version') != 'crossstage-1': self.error('不是crossstage-1契约')
        if not self.r.get('month') or not self.r.get('policy_version'): self.error('缺月份或规则版本')
        self.load_sources(); self.load_tables(); self.scope(); self.stage_rows(); self.check_facts()
        self.raw_scope_dispositions(); self.raw_attendance(); self.raw_rewards(); self.raw_insurance(); self.template_receipts(); self.comment_business_review(); self.attendance_handoff_review(); self.confirmations(); self.maternity_handoff()
        self.errors = list(dict.fromkeys(self.errors)); self.pending = list(dict.fromkeys(self.pending))
        mode = self.r.get('mode', 'draft')
        if mode not in ('draft', 'formal'): self.error('模式须为draft/formal')
        purpose=self.r.get('purpose','template_delivery')
        if purpose not in ('template_delivery','calculation_details'):self.error('purpose须为template_delivery/calculation_details')
        required=self.required_delivery_stages()
        verified=[x for x in required if self.evidence.get('template_receipts',{}).get(x,{}).get('technical_verified')]
        technical_ready=bool(required) and set(required)<=set(verified) and not self.technical_errors and not self.technical_pending
        data_errors=[x for x in self.errors if x not in self.technical_errors]
        data_pending=[x for x in self.pending if x not in self.technical_pending]
        calculation_passed=not data_errors; business_ready=calculation_passed and not data_pending
        formal_ready=purpose=='template_delivery' and business_ready and technical_ready
        passed=calculation_passed and (mode=='draft' or business_ready) and (purpose=='calculation_details' or technical_ready)
        status='calculation_details_only' if purpose=='calculation_details' else ('technical_incomplete' if not technical_ready else ('formal_template_verified' if mode=='formal' and formal_ready else 'template_draft_technically_verified'))
        message='仅计算明细，未交付合规模板' if purpose=='calculation_details' else ('模板技术交付未完成；可算独立明细已保留' if not technical_ready else '本次实际交付范围技术已核验，金额/政策待核另列')
        return {'passed':passed,'calculation_passed':calculation_passed,'business_ready':business_ready,'formal_ready':formal_ready,'purpose':purpose,'delivery_status':status,'delivery_message':message,'technical_delivery':{'ready':technical_ready,'required_template_stages':required,'verified_template_stages':verified,'all_five_stages_verified':set(('attendance','insurance','payroll','tax','payslip'))<=set(verified),'input_only_stages':[x for x in getattr(self,'stages',()) if x in ('attendance','insurance','payroll','tax','payslip') and x not in required],'errors':self.technical_errors,'pending':self.technical_pending},'mode':mode,'month':self.r.get('month'),'employee_count':len(self.people),'errors':self.errors,'pending':self.pending,'evidence':self.evidence}

def audit_crossstage(record, base_dir=Path('.')):
    try: return Audit(record, base_dir).run()
    except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError, IndexError) as exc:
        return {'passed': False, 'formal_ready': False, 'errors': ['输入契约无效:' + str(exc)], 'pending': []}

def write_scope_review_csv(report, path):
    path = Path(path)
    if path.exists(): raise ValueError('范围复核CSV已存在，不得覆盖')
    fields = ('name','route','reason','original_table','original_rows')
    with path.open('w', encoding='utf-8-sig', newline='') as stream:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=fields); writer.writeheader()
        for row in report.get('evidence', {}).get('scope_review', []):
            writer.writerow({key: ';'.join(map(str,row.get(key,[]))) if key=='original_rows' else row.get(key,'') for key in fields})

def write_comment_review_csv(report,path):
    path=Path(path)
    if path.exists():raise ValueError('原工资批注业务复核CSV已存在，不得覆盖')
    fields=('name','employee_key','source_path','source_sheet','source_cell','original_text','object_status','action','effective_status','reason')
    with path.open('w',encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as stream:
        writer=csv.DictWriter(stream,fieldnames=fields);writer.writeheader()
        for row in report.get('evidence',{}).get('comment_business_review',{}).get('items',[]):writer.writerow({key:row.get(key,'') for key in fields})

def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--record', type=Path, required=True); ap.add_argument('--output', type=Path, required=True)
    a = ap.parse_args(); record = json.loads(a.record.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
    protected = {a.record.resolve()} | {(a.record.parent / str(s.get('path', ''))).resolve() for s in record.get('sources', {}).values()}
    if a.output.resolve() in protected or a.output.exists(): raise SystemExit('输出不得覆盖记录、来源或既有结果')
    result = audit_crossstage(record, a.record.parent)
    a.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    scope_csv = a.output.with_suffix('.scope.csv')
    if scope_csv.resolve() in protected: raise SystemExit('范围复核CSV不得覆盖来源')
    write_scope_review_csv(result, scope_csv); result['scope_review_csv'] = str(scope_csv)
    comment_csv=a.output.with_suffix('.comments.csv')
    if comment_csv.resolve() in protected:raise SystemExit('批注复核CSV不得覆盖来源')
    write_comment_review_csv(result,comment_csv);result['comment_review_csv']=str(comment_csv)
    a.output.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str), encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps({k:result.get(k) for k in ('passed','calculation_passed','formal_ready','purpose','delivery_status','delivery_message','technical_delivery','errors','pending')},ensure_ascii=False))
    raise SystemExit(0 if result['passed'] else 2)

if __name__=='__main__':
    from diagnostics_bridge import observed
    observed(main,'crossstage_audit')
`````

## skills/payroll-operations/scripts/crossstage_scaffold.py

`````python
"""生成可执行的跨阶段记录；真实输入只计算能独立证实的考勤事实。
演示：python crossstage_scaffold.py --demo --output-dir 全新目录
真实：--scope 实际名单.json --month YYYY-MM [--attendance-config 映射.json] --output-dir 全新目录
"""
import argparse
import calendar
import json
from pathlib import Path
from crossstage_audit import audit_crossstage, sha256, REQUIRED, LEAVE_ITEMS, write_scope_review_csv

def save(path, value):
    if path.exists(): raise ValueError('不得覆盖既有输出:' + str(path))
    path.write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')

def add_table(record, directory, table_id, rows, role='calculation'):
    path = directory / (table_id + '.json'); save(path, rows)
    record['sources'][table_id] = {'path': str(path.resolve()), 'sha256': sha256(path), 'month': record['month'], 'role': role}
    columns = {k: k for row in rows for k in row}
    record['tables'][table_id] = {'source_id': table_id, 'format': 'json', 'start_row': 1, 'end_row': max(1, len(rows)), 'columns': columns}

def make_record(directory, month, policy_version):
    directory = Path(directory).resolve()
    if directory.exists(): raise ValueError('请指定尚不存在的新目录')
    directory.mkdir(parents=True)
    return directory, {'schema_version': 'crossstage-1', 'month': month, 'policy_version': policy_version, 'mode': 'draft', 'purpose': 'calculation_details', 'through_stage': 'attendance', 'scope_table': 'scope', 'sources': {}, 'tables': {}, 'stages': {}, 'facts': [], 'confirmations': []}

def finish(directory, record):
    save(directory / 'run.json', record)
    report = audit_crossstage(record, directory)
    write_scope_review_csv(report, directory / 'scope-review.csv')
    report['scope_review_csv'] = str(directory / 'scope-review.csv')
    save(directory / 'audit.json', report)
    return {'record': str(directory / 'run.json'), 'report': str(directory / 'audit.json'), 'passed': report['passed'], 'formal_ready': report['formal_ready'], 'purpose': report['purpose'], 'delivery_status': report['delivery_status'], 'delivery_message': report['delivery_message'], 'technical_delivery': report['technical_delivery'], 'errors': report['errors'], 'pending_count': len(report['pending'])}

def demo(output_dir):
    """全部为明确合成数据，无任何真实确认。"""
    directory, record = make_record(output_dir, '2026-09', 'synthetic-demonstration-only')
    person = {'employee_key': 'SYNTHETIC-1', 'name': '明确合成人员', 'company': '明确合成公司'}
    add_table(record, directory, 'scope', [person], 'scope_approved')
    add_table(record, directory, 'daily', [dict(person, date=f'2026-09-{d:02}', attendance_type='需要打卡', **{k: 0 for k in LEAVE_ITEMS}) for d in range(1, 31)], 'raw')
    record['raw_attendance'] = {'table': 'daily', 'leave_columns': {k: {'kind': k, 'unit': 'hours'} for k in LEAVE_ITEMS}}
    save(directory / 'attendance-config.json', {'path': str((directory / 'daily.json').resolve()), 'table': record['tables']['daily'], 'raw_attendance': record['raw_attendance']})
    add_table(record, directory, 'claims', [dict(person, date='2026-09-01', tier=50, next_workday=True, checkout='22:01', next_arrival='08:59')], 'raw')
    record['raw_rewards'] = {'table': 'claims'}
    insurance = dict(person, month='2026-09', medical_employer_mode='combined')
    for k, amount in zip(REQUIRED['insurance'], (100, 10, 20, 30)):
        insurance[k + '_employee'] = amount; insurance[k + '_employer'] = amount * 2
    add_table(record, directory, 'insurance_raw', [insurance], 'raw')
    record['raw_insurance'] = {'table': 'insurance_raw', 'components': {k: {'employee': k + '_employee', 'employer': k + '_employer'} for k in REQUIRED['insurance']}}
    add_table(record, directory, 'salary_inputs', [dict(person, salary=10000, salary_adjustment=0, progression=0, performance=0, manual_adjustments=0)], 'manual')
    record['through_stage'] = 'payslip'
    for stage, required_items in REQUIRED.items():
        items = required_items + ('finance', 'bank', 'third_party', 'cash_other') if stage == 'payroll' else required_items
        row = dict(person, **{item: 0 for item in items})
        if 'reward' in items: row['reward'] = 50
        if stage == 'insurance': row.update({k: insurance[k + '_employee'] for k in items})
        if stage == 'payroll': row.update(salary=10000, personal_social=110, personal_medical=20, personal_fund=30, employer_social=220, employer_medical=40, employer_fund=60, gross=10050, finance=10050, net=9890, bank=9890)
        if stage == 'tax': row.update(taxable=10050, personal_social=110, personal_medical=20, personal_fund=30)
        if stage == 'payslip': row.update(gross=10050, net=9890)
        add_table(record, directory, stage, [row])
        record['stages'][stage] = {'table': stage, 'dispositions': [{'employee_key': person['employee_key'], 'route': 'output', 'row': 1}]}
        for item in items:
            ref = {'table': stage, 'row': 1, 'column': item}
            fact = {'employee_key': person['employee_key'], 'stage': stage, 'item': item, 'status': 'calculated', 'amount': row[item], 'source_refs': [ref], 'value_refs': [ref]}
            if item in LEAVE_ITEMS.values(): fact.update(observed_rows=[], unit='hours')
            if stage == 'insurance': fact['employer_amount'] = insurance[item + '_employer']
            if stage == 'reward': fact['claim_rows'] = [1]
            if stage == 'payroll' and item in ('salary', 'salary_adjustment', 'progression', 'performance', 'manual_adjustments'):
                ref = {'table': 'salary_inputs', 'row': 1, 'column': item}
                fact['source_refs'] = [ref]; fact['derivation'] = {'operation': 'copy', 'refs': [ref]}
            record['facts'].append(fact)
    return finish(directory, record)

def scaffold(scope, month, output_dir, attendance_config=None, policy_version='待核定规则版本'):
    directory, record = make_record(output_dir, month, policy_version)
    scope = Path(scope).resolve()
    rows = json.loads(scope.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or not rows: raise ValueError('名单须为非空JSON对象数组')
    if any(not all(p.get(k) for k in ('employee_key', 'name', 'company')) for p in rows): raise ValueError('名单须提供employee_key/name/company')
    record['sources']['scope'] = {'path': str(scope), 'sha256': sha256(scope), 'month': month, 'role': 'scope_approved'}
    record['tables']['scope'] = {'source_id': 'scope', 'format': 'json', 'start_row': 1, 'end_row': len(rows), 'columns': {k: k for p in rows for k in p}}
    record['stages']['attendance'] = {'dispositions': [{'employee_key': p['employee_key'], 'route': 'pending', 'reason': '待逐项核算；不得仅因缺资料删除本人'} for p in rows]}
    if attendance_config:
        config_path = Path(attendance_config).resolve()
        config = json.loads(config_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
        source = Path(config['path'])
        if not source.is_absolute(): source = config_path.parent / source
        record['sources']['daily'] = {'path': str(source.resolve()), 'sha256': sha256(source), 'month': month, 'role': 'raw'}
        record['tables']['daily'] = dict(config['table'], source_id='daily')
        record['raw_attendance'] = dict(config['raw_attendance'], table='daily')
    # 先读取实际全范围观察；尚无事实时不会捏造金额，身份/映射错误仍保留错误。
    observed = audit_crossstage(record, directory).get('evidence', {}).get('raw_attendance', {})
    calculated = []
    for p in rows:
        obs = observed.get(p['employee_key'], {})
        output = dict(p, **{item: None for item in REQUIRED['attendance']})
        for item in REQUIRED['attendance']:
            fact = {'employee_key': p['employee_key'], 'stage': 'attendance', 'item': item, 'status': 'pending', 'amount': None, 'reason': '缺对应原始依据，其他可算项目继续'}
            kind = next((k for k, v in LEAVE_ITEMS.items() if v == item), None)
            if kind:
                leaves = obs.get('leave', {}).get(kind, [])
                fact.update(unit='hours', observed_rows=[{'row': x['row'], 'column': x['column']} for x in leaves])
                complete = obs.get('coverage_complete') and obs.get('types') in (['需要打卡'], ['不需要打卡']) and not any(x['row'] in obs.get('rows', []) for x in obs.get('unknown', []))
                if kind in ('personal', 'sick') and obs.get('types') == ['不需要打卡']: complete = False
                if complete:
                    amount = float(obs['business_hours'][kind]); output[item] = amount
                    first = obs['rows'][0]
                    fact.update(status='calculated', amount=amount, reason='从完整实际日明细独立计算', source_refs=[{'table': 'daily', 'row': x['row'], 'column': x['column']} for x in leaves] or [{'table': 'daily', 'row': first, 'column': 'date'}])
            record['facts'].append(fact)
        calculated.append(output)
    add_table(record, directory, 'attendance_calculation', calculated)
    record['stages']['attendance']['table'] = 'attendance_calculation'
    for n, disposition in enumerate(record['stages']['attendance']['dispositions'], 1): disposition['row'] = n
    for f in record['facts']:
        n = next(i for i, p in enumerate(rows, 1) if p['employee_key'] == f['employee_key'])
        f['value_refs'] = [{'table': 'attendance_calculation', 'row': n, 'column': f['item']}]
    return finish(directory, record)

def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(); group = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    group.add_argument('--demo', action='store_true'); group.add_argument('--scope', type=Path)
    ap.add_argument('--month'); ap.add_argument('--output-dir', type=Path, required=True); ap.add_argument('--attendance-config', type=Path); ap.add_argument('--policy-version', default='待核定规则版本')
    a = ap.parse_args()
    if a.scope and not a.month: ap.error('真实名单模式必须指定--month')
    try: result = demo(a.output_dir) if a.demo else scaffold(a.scope, a.month, a.output_dir, a.attendance_config, a.policy_version)
    except (ValueError, KeyError, OSError) as exc: ap.error(str(exc))
    print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False)); raise SystemExit(0 if result['passed'] else 2)

if __name__ == '__main__': main()
`````

## skills/payroll-operations/scripts/diagnostics_bridge.py

`````python
"""已告知的自动诊断入口；诊断失败不改变核算结果。"""
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,sys,time

def observed(function,entry):
    started=time.time();code=0;kind=None
    try:
        value=function()
        if isinstance(value,int):code=value
        return value
    except SystemExit as exc:
        code=exc.code if isinstance(exc.code,int) else (0 if exc.code is None else 2);raise
    except BaseException as exc:code=2;kind=type(exc).__name__;raise
    finally:
        root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
        component=root/'payroll-diagnostics'/'collector.py'
        if not component.is_file():component=root/'diagnostics'/'collector.py'
        home=Path(os.environ.get('CODEX_HOME',str(Path.home()/'.codex'))).resolve()/'payroll-diagnostics-data'
        if component.is_file() and (home/'client.secure').is_file():
            allowed={'--record','--output','--month','--workbook','--attendance','--roster','--history','--overtime'}
            selected=[];args=sys.argv[1:]
            for index,flag in enumerate(args[:-1]):
                if flag in allowed:selected.extend([flag,args[index+1]])
            import json
            command=[sys.executable,str(component),'record','--home',str(home),'--entry',entry,'--started',str(started),'--exit-code',str(code),'--argument-list',json.dumps(selected,ensure_ascii=False)]
            if kind:command.extend(['--exception-kind',kind])
            try:subprocess.run(command,stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,timeout=90,creationflags=0x08000000 if os.name=='nt' else 0)
            except Exception:pass
`````

## skills/payroll-operations/scripts/legacy_template_guard.py

`````python
"""只读核对政府原始 .xls 模板，不转换格式、不制作工资、不冒称公式已复核。

按用户明确例外可允许原始黄色字段清底色；此例外不允许其他字体/边框/列宽漂移。
"""
import argparse
from copy import deepcopy
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import sys

import xlrd
from template_guard import sha256, digest, write_json


def plain(obj):
    if isinstance(obj, (str, int, float, bool)) or obj is None:
        return obj
    if isinstance(obj, (tuple, list)):
        return [plain(v) for v in obj]
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        return {str(k): plain(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
    return {k: plain(v) for k, v in vars(obj).items() if not k.startswith('_')}


def cell_style(book, sheet, row, col):
    xf = book.xf_list[sheet.cell_xf_index(row, col)]
    return {key: plain(getattr(xf, key)) for key in ('alignment', 'border', 'background', 'protection')} | {
        'font': plain(book.font_list[xf.font_index]),
        'number_format': book.format_map[xf.format_key].format_str}


def yellow(style, book):
    background = style['background']
    rgb = book.colour_map.get(background['pattern_colour_index'])
    return bool(background['fill_pattern'] and rgb and rgb[0] >= 200 and rgb[1] >= 170 and rgb[2] < 170)


def print_records(path):
    """保留 BIFF 打印/页边距/页眉页脚/分页记录，不用表头代替打印核对。"""
    from xlrd.compdoc import CompDoc
    document = CompDoc(Path(path).read_bytes())
    stream = document.get_named_stream('Workbook') or document.get_named_stream('Book')
    codes = {0xA1, 0x26, 0x27, 0x28, 0x29, 0x14, 0x15, 0x2B, 0x2A, 0x83, 0x84, 0x1A, 0x1B}
    records, offset = [], 0
    while offset + 4 <= len(stream):
        code, length = struct.unpack_from('<HH', stream, offset)
        data = stream[offset+4:offset+4+length]
        if code in codes:
            records.append([code, data.hex()])
        offset += 4 + length
    return records


def snapshot(book):
    result = {}
    for sheet in book.sheets():
        result[sheet.name] = {
            'shape': [sheet.nrows, sheet.ncols], 'visibility': sheet.visibility,
            'merges': plain(sheet.merged_cells),
            'row_dimensions': plain(sheet.rowinfo_map), 'column_dimensions': plain(sheet.colinfo_map),
            'default_col_width': sheet.defcolwidth, 'default_row_height': sheet.default_row_height,
            'cells': {f'{r+1},{c+1}': {'value': sheet.cell_value(r, c),
                'type': sheet.cell_type(r, c), 'style': cell_style(book, sheet, r, c)}
                for r in range(sheet.nrows) for c in range(sheet.ncols)}}
    return result


def row_settings(sheet,row):
    info=sheet.rowinfo_map.get(row)
    return {'height':info.height if info is not None and not info.has_default_height else sheet.default_row_height,
        'hidden':bool(info.hidden) if info else False,'outline_level':info.outline_level if info else 0}


def inspect(template, expected_sha256, report=None, output=None, clear_original_yellow=False,
            clear_all_fills=False, personnel_prototype_rows=None):
    template = Path(template).resolve()
    if template.suffix.lower() != '.xls' or sha256(template) != expected_sha256:
        raise ValueError('须为用户指定原始 .xls，且 SHA 与预先固定值一致')
    source = xlrd.open_workbook(template, formatting_info=True)
    original = snapshot(source)
    yellow_fields, yellow_cells = {}, {}
    for sheet in source.sheets():
        yellow_cells[sheet.name] = [f'{r+1},{c+1}' for r in range(sheet.nrows) for c in range(sheet.ncols)
                                    if yellow(cell_style(source, sheet, r, c), source)]
        yellow_fields[sheet.name] = [{'column': c+1, 'field': sheet.cell_value(0, c)}
            for c in range(sheet.ncols) if sheet.nrows and yellow(cell_style(source, sheet, 0, c), source)]
    errors, checks = [], []
    row_mapping=[]
    body=source.sheet_by_index(0)
    prototypes=list(range(2,body.nrows+1)) if personnel_prototype_rows is None else list(personnel_prototype_rows)
    if not prototypes or any(isinstance(r,bool) or not isinstance(r,int) or not 2<=r<=body.nrows for r in prototypes):
        raise ValueError('合法人员原型行须为原业务页第2行起的实际行，不得使用表头/说明页')
    if source.sheet_names() != ['正常工资薪金收入', '填表说明'] or source.sheet_by_index(0).ncols != 22:
        errors.append('本案例政府模板须为22列且保留正常工资薪金收入/填表说明两张表')
    if output is not None:
        output = Path(output).resolve()
        if output == template or output.suffix.lower() != '.xls':
            raise ValueError('输出须另存原始 .xls，不得覆盖模板或转换 .xlsx')
        book = xlrd.open_workbook(output, formatting_info=True)
        actual = snapshot(book)
        if book.sheet_names() != source.sheet_names():
            errors.append('政府模板工作表名称/说明页缺失或改变')
        else:
            original_notes, actual_notes = deepcopy(original['填表说明']), deepcopy(actual['填表说明'])
            if clear_all_fills:
                for note in (original_notes, actual_notes):
                    for cell in note['cells'].values():
                        cell['style'].pop('background')
                for cell in actual['填表说明']['cells'].values():
                    if cell['style']['background']['fill_pattern']:
                        errors.append('填表说明仍有底色，未完成全部清底色')
            if actual_notes != original_notes:
                errors.append('政府模板填表说明的文字、样式或结构改变')
            name = source.sheet_names()[0]
            src, dest = source.sheet_by_name(name), book.sheet_by_name(name)
            if dest.ncols != 22 or dest.row_values(0) != src.row_values(0):
                errors.append('政府模板22列或字段顺序改变')
            for key in ('column_dimensions', 'merges', 'visibility', 'default_col_width', 'default_row_height'):
                if original[name][key] != actual[name][key]:
                    errors.append(f'政府模板参数改变：{key}')
            yellow_columns = {item['column']-1 for item in yellow_fields[name]}
            for r in range(dest.nrows):
                def comparable_style(b,s,row,col):
                    value=cell_style(b,s,row,col)
                    if clear_all_fills or clear_original_yellow and col in yellow_columns:
                        value=deepcopy(value);value.pop('background')
                    return value
                candidates=[r] if r<src.nrows else [n-1 for n in prototypes]
                def row_score(sr):
                    return sum(comparable_style(source,src,sr,c)!=comparable_style(book,dest,r,c)
                               for c in range(min(dest.ncols,src.ncols)))
                exact=[sr for sr in candidates if row_score(sr)==0]
                height_exact=[sr for sr in exact if row_settings(src,sr)==row_settings(dest,r)]
                source_r=height_exact[0] if height_exact else exact[0] if exact else min(candidates,key=row_score)
                if row_settings(src,source_r)!=row_settings(dest,r):
                    errors.append(f'政府模板人员行高度/隐藏/分组与完整来源原型不符：{name}!行{r+1}，'
                        f'原型行{source_r+1}={row_settings(src,source_r)}，成品={row_settings(dest,r)}')
                row_mapping.append({'output_row':r+1,'template_row':source_r+1,
                    'added_personnel_row':r>=src.nrows,'template_row_settings':row_settings(src,source_r),
                    'actual_row_settings':row_settings(dest,r)})
                for c in range(min(dest.ncols, src.ncols)):
                    expected, current = cell_style(source, src, source_r, c), cell_style(book, dest, r, c)
                    allowed = clear_all_fills or clear_original_yellow and c in yellow_columns
                    if allowed:
                        if current['background']['fill_pattern'] != 0:
                            errors.append(f'核定清底色范围仍有填充：{name}!{r+1},{c+1}')
                        expected, current = deepcopy(expected), deepcopy(current)
                        expected.pop('background'); current.pop('background')
                    if expected != current:
                        errors.append(f'政府模板非核定样式变化：{name}!{r+1},{c+1}')
                    if len(errors) >= 50:
                        break
                if len(errors) >= 50:
                    break
            if print_records(template) != print_records(output):
                errors.append('政府模板打印/页边距/页眉页脚/分页记录改变')
            checks = ['原始22列与顺序', '完整说明页', '逐格字体/边框/数字格式/对齐/填充', '列尺寸/合并/打印BIFF记录',
                      '新增人员整行样式与有效行高绑定合法原型（排除表头说明）']
    result = {'schema': 'payroll-legacy-template-guard/1', 'passed': not errors, 'errors': errors,
              'template': {'path': str(template), 'sha256': expected_sha256},
              'template_fingerprint_sha256': digest(original), 'original_yellow_fields': yellow_fields,
              'original_yellow_cells': yellow_cells, 'clear_original_yellow_allowed': clear_original_yellow,
              'clear_all_fills_allowed': clear_all_fills,
              'personnel_prototype_rows':prototypes,'personnel_row_mapping':row_mapping,
              'checks': checks, 'native_recalculation': False, 'formal_ready': False,
              'status': 'legacy_structure_pending_native_formula_audit' if output else 'legacy_template_read_only_inspected',
              'limitations': ['xlrd仅取得公式缓存，不能证明公式保留或原生重算；须另用Excel只读公式核对',
                              '数据行金额和人员是否正确须由核定来源及跨环节审计检查，本工具不证明这些业务事实']}
    if output:
        result['output'] = {'path': str(output), 'sha256': sha256(output)}
        result['finalized_output_sha256'] = result['output']['sha256']
    if report is not None:
        write_json(report, result)
    return result


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--template', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--expected-sha256', required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--report', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--clear-original-yellow', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--clear-all-fills', action='store_true', help='先识别原始黄色必填字段，再检查所有工作表全部单元格无底色')
    parser.add_argument('--personnel-prototype-rows',type=lambda value:[int(x) for x in value.split(',')],
        help='预先核定的1起算人员原型行，如2,3；默认原业务页第2行起，排除表头/说明')
    try:
        result = inspect(**vars(parser.parse_args()))
        print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
        return 0 if result['passed'] else 2
    except (ValueError, KeyError, OSError, xlrd.XLRDError) as exc:
        print(json.dumps({'status': 'rejected', 'error': str(exc)}, ensure_ascii=False)); return 2


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
`````

## skills/payroll-operations/scripts/native_recalc_verify.py

`````python
"""只在隔离副本执行真实Excel复核；不接收外部passed作为证据。
先核ProgID注册及实际进程的厂商/文件身份，记录真实引擎，无法证实则待核。
"""
import hashlib
import json
import math
import re
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
from zipfile import ZipFile
from xml.etree import ElementTree as ET

def sha256(path): return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def same(a, b):
    if isinstance(a, (int, float)) and not isinstance(a, bool) and isinstance(b, (int, float)) and not isinstance(b, bool):
        return math.isfinite(a) and math.isfinite(b) and abs(a-b) <= max(0.000001, abs(a)*0.000000001)
    return a == b

def observe_cell(cell):
    """保存真实显示及ISERROR类型；####仅表示显示不足，不能当计算错误。"""
    value=cell.Value2
    try:text=str(cell.Text or '')
    except Exception:text=None
    formula=bool(cell.HasFormula);is_error=None;method='unavailable'
    try:
        address=cell.Address;address=address(False,False) if callable(address) else str(address)
        detected=cell.Parent.Evaluate('ISERROR('+address+')')
        if isinstance(detected,bool):is_error=detected;method='worksheet_ISERROR'
    except Exception:pass
    if is_error is None:
        # pywin32可把VT_ERROR暴露为有符号COM错误码；须同时有错误显示证据。
        error_text=bool(re.fullmatch(r'#(?:NULL!|DIV/0!|VALUE!|REF!|NAME\?|NUM!|N/A|GETTING_DATA|SPILL!|CALC!|FIELD!|BLOCKED!|UNKNOWN!|CONNECT!|BUSY!)',text or '',re.I))
        if isinstance(value,int) and not isinstance(value,bool) and (value & 0xFFFF0000)==0x800A0000 and error_text:
            is_error=True;method='COM_error_code_and_display'
    return {'value':value,'text':text,'has_formula':formula,'is_error':is_error,'error_type_source':method}

def assess_linkage_output(before,after,expected):
    """先判真实技术错误，再判未知预期；未知金额不能掩盖公式错误。"""
    if before['is_error'] is True or after['is_error'] is True:return 'failed','实际联动输出含Excel公式错误'
    if not after['has_formula']:return 'failed','联动输出不是实际公式'
    if before['value'] in (None,'') and after['value'] in (None,''):return 'pending','联动输出仍为空白，其他依赖或口径未决，未证实完整联动'
    if expected is None:return 'pending','缺核定数值预期，不能用本机计算结果反推未知规则'
    if same(before['value'],after['value']) or not (after['value'] in (None,'') if expected=='' else same(after['value'],expected)):return 'failed','实际公式未按核定预期联动'
    return 'verified',None

def formula_cache_metadata(worksheet_xml):
    """空字符串缓存须同时有公式、t=str及v节点，区别于尚未计算的空v/无v。"""
    ns = '{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main}'
    result = {}
    for cell in ET.fromstring(worksheet_xml).findall('.//' + ns + 'c'):
        if cell.find(ns + 'f') is None: continue
        value = cell.find(ns + 'v')
        empty_string = value is not None and cell.get('t') == 'str' and value.text in (None, '')
        result[cell.get('r')] = {'xml_type': cell.get('t'), 'has_v': value is not None, 'empty_string_cache': empty_string, 'cache_present': value is not None and (empty_string or value.text not in (None, ''))}
    return result

def engine_identity(path):
    import win32api
    path = Path(path)
    if not path.is_file(): return None
    translations = win32api.GetFileVersionInfo(str(path), '\\VarFileInfo\\Translation')
    for language, encoding in translations:
        try: company = win32api.GetFileVersionInfo(str(path), f'\\StringFileInfo\\{language:04x}{encoding:04x}\\CompanyName')
        except Exception: continue
        try: original = win32api.GetFileVersionInfo(str(path), f'\\StringFileInfo\\{language:04x}{encoding:04x}\\OriginalFilename')
        except Exception: original = path.name
        if 'Microsoft' in company and original.upper() == 'EXCEL.EXE' and path.name.upper() == 'EXCEL.EXE': return 'Microsoft Excel'
        if any(text in company.lower() for text in ('kingsoft', '金山')) and path.name.upper() in ('ET.EXE', 'WPS.EXE', 'KSOLAUNCH.EXE', 'WPSOFFICE.EXE'): return 'WPS Office'
    return None

def registered_engine():
    """优先让COM解析ProgID，再核32/64注册视图；缺键不代表引擎不存在。"""
    import pythoncom
    import winreg
    result = {'requested_progid': 'Excel.Application', 'registered_executable': None, 'registration_gaps': []}
    try:
        clsid = str(pythoncom.CLSIDFromProgID(result['requested_progid'])); result['clsid_resolver'] = 'pythoncom'
    except Exception as exc:
        result['registration_gaps'].append('pythoncom未能预先解析ProgID:' + str(exc))
        try:
            import ctypes
            import uuid
            ole32 = ctypes.OleDLL('ole32')
            identifier = (ctypes.c_ubyte * 16)()
            ole32.CLSIDFromProgID.argtypes = [ctypes.c_wchar_p, ctypes.c_void_p]
            ole32.CLSIDFromProgID(result['requested_progid'], ctypes.byref(identifier))
            clsid = '{' + str(uuid.UUID(bytes_le=bytes(identifier))).upper() + '}'
            result['clsid_resolver'] = 'ole32.CLSIDFromProgID'
        except Exception as fallback:
            result['registration_gaps'].append('Windows COM解析也未完成:' + str(fallback)); return result
    result['resolved_clsid'] = clsid
    for view in (winreg.KEY_WOW64_32KEY, winreg.KEY_WOW64_64KEY):
        try:
            with winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CLASSES_ROOT, 'CLSID\\' + clsid + r'\LocalServer32', 0, winreg.KEY_READ | view) as key: command = winreg.QueryValue(key, None)
            text = str(command).strip()
            executable = text.split('"')[1] if text.startswith('"') else text[:text.lower().index('.exe')+4]
            if Path(executable).is_file():
                result.update(registered_executable=str(Path(executable).resolve()), registration_view=view); return result
            result['registration_gaps'].append('注册命令没有可核实执行档:' + str(view))
        except (OSError, ValueError, IndexError) as exc: result['registration_gaps'].append('注册视图定位缺口:' + str(view) + ':' + str(exc))
    return result

def process_engine(window):
    import ctypes
    from ctypes import wintypes
    import win32process
    _, pid = win32process.GetWindowThreadProcessId(int(window))
    kernel = ctypes.WinDLL('kernel32', use_last_error=True)
    kernel.OpenProcess.argtypes = [wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.BOOL, wintypes.DWORD]
    kernel.OpenProcess.restype = wintypes.HANDLE
    kernel.QueryFullProcessImageNameW.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE, wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.LPWSTR, ctypes.POINTER(wintypes.DWORD)]
    kernel.CloseHandle.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]
    handle = kernel.OpenProcess(0x1000, False, pid)
    if not handle: raise OSError('无法读取实际Excel进程身份')
    try:
        size = wintypes.DWORD(32768); buffer = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(size.value)
        if not kernel.QueryFullProcessImageNameW(handle, 0, buffer, ctypes.byref(size)): raise OSError('无法解析实际Excel进程路径')
        return pid, buffer.value
    finally: kernel.CloseHandle(handle)

def native_child(config):
    result = {'verified': False, 'errors': [], 'pending': [], 'formula_cache_count': 0, 'linkage_checks': []}
    app = book = None; independent = False; original_settings = {}; mutex = None; kernel = None
    try:
        import ctypes
        from ctypes import wintypes
        kernel = ctypes.WinDLL('kernel32', use_last_error=True)
        kernel.CreateMutexW.argtypes = [wintypes.LPVOID, wintypes.BOOL, wintypes.LPCWSTR]
        kernel.CreateMutexW.restype = wintypes.HANDLE
        kernel.WaitForSingleObject.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE, wintypes.DWORD]
        kernel.ReleaseMutex.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]; kernel.CloseHandle.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]
        mutex = kernel.CreateMutexW(None, False, 'Local\\CodexPayrollNativeRecalcV1')
        if not mutex or kernel.WaitForSingleObject(mutex, 0) not in (0, 0x80):
            result['pending'].append('原生复核正在被另一任务使用，本次未打开工作簿')
            if mutex: kernel.CloseHandle(mutex)
            mutex = None; return result
        registration = registered_engine(); result.update(registration)
        executable = registration['registered_executable']
        if executable:
            try: result['registered_engine'] = engine_identity(executable)
            except Exception as exc: result['registration_gaps'].append('注册执行档版本资料无法读取:' + str(exc))
        # 注册定位不足时仍可在未开工作簿之前核实际进程，不凭缺键推断没有引擎。
        import win32com.client
        import win32process
        import win32api
        before_processes = set(win32process.EnumProcesses()); result['process_count_before_dispatch'] = len(before_processes)
        app = win32com.client.DispatchEx('Excel.Application')
        pid, actual = process_engine(app.Hwnd); result.update(process_id=pid, actual_executable=actual)
        independent = pid not in before_processes
        initial_books = int(app.Workbooks.Count)
        result.update(independent_new_process=independent, workbooks_before_open=initial_books, executable_sha256=sha256(actual), executable_version=win32api.GetFileVersionInfo(actual, '\\'))
        engine_name = engine_identity(actual); result['actual_engine'] = engine_name
        if not engine_name:
            result['pending'].append('实际进程身份无法证实；未打开工作簿')
            return result
        result['microsoft_excel'] = engine_name == 'Microsoft Excel'
        if initial_books:
            result['pending'].append('该办公实例已有其他工作簿，本次未打开隔离文件'); return result
        for setting in ('Visible', 'DisplayAlerts', 'EnableEvents', 'AskToUpdateLinks', 'AutomationSecurity', 'Calculation'):
            try: original_settings[setting] = getattr(app, setting)
            except Exception: pass
        if independent: app.Visible = False
        app.DisplayAlerts = False; app.EnableEvents = False; app.AskToUpdateLinks = False
        app.AutomationSecurity = 3
        try: app.Calculation = -4135
        except Exception: pass
        # 配置只含事先复制的隔离文件，子进程没有原件路径。
        book = app.Workbooks.Open(str(Path(config['isolated_path']).resolve()), UpdateLinks=0, ReadOnly=False)
        try: app.Calculation = -4135
        except Exception: pass
        formulas = config['formulas']
        if config['format'] == 'xls':
            for sheet in book.Worksheets:
                for cell in sheet.UsedRange:
                    if cell.HasFormula:
                        address = cell.Address(False, False)
                        formulas.append({'sheet': sheet.Name, 'cell': address, 'formula': str(cell.Formula), 'cached': config['xls_values'].get(sheet.Name, {}).get(address),'cached_error':config.get('xls_errors',{}).get(sheet.Name,{}).get(address)})
        app.CalculateFullRebuild()
        if int(app.CalculationState) != 0: result['pending'].append('原生计算尚未结束')
        for item in formulas:
            cell = book.Worksheets(item['sheet']).Range(item['cell'])
            cache_present = item.get('cache_present', item['cached'] is not None)
            observation=observe_cell(cell);actual=observation['value']
            if item.get('xml_type')=='e' or item.get('cached_error'):
                result['errors'].append('实际原文件公式缓存为Excel错误:'+item['sheet']+'!'+item['cell']+':'+str(item.get('cached_error') or item.get('cached')))
            if observation['is_error'] is True:
                result['errors'].append('实际原生重算为Excel公式错误:'+item['sheet']+'!'+item['cell']+':'+str(observation['text']))
            if item.get('xml_type')=='e' or item.get('cached_error') or observation['is_error'] is True:
                result.setdefault('formula_errors',[]).append({'sheet':item['sheet'],'cell':item['cell'],'cached':item.get('cached'),'cache_xml_type':item.get('xml_type'),'native_observation':observation})
            empty_string = item.get('empty_string_cache', item['cached'] == '')
            if not cache_present or not (actual in (None, '') if empty_string else same(item['cached'], actual)):
                result['errors'].append('实际原文件公式缓存缺失或与原生重算不符:' + item['sheet'] + '!' + item['cell'])
            if empty_string and cache_present: result['typed_empty_cache_count'] = result.get('typed_empty_cache_count', 0) + 1
            result['formula_cache_count'] += 1
        for check in config['linkage_checks']:
            sheet = book.Worksheets(check['sheet']); target = sheet.Range(check['input_cell'])
            if target.HasFormula or not same(target.Value2, check['input_expected']) or same(check['test_value'], check['input_expected']):
                result['errors'].append('联动输入不是核定可编辑原值或测试值未变化'); continue
            output_key=lambda item:item.get('sheet',check['sheet'])+'!'+item['cell']
            output_cell=lambda item:book.Worksheets(item.get('sheet',check['sheet'])).Range(item['cell'])
            before = {output_key(item):observe_cell(output_cell(item)) for item in check['outputs']}
            target.Value2 = check['test_value']; app.CalculateFullRebuild()
            output_results = []
            for item in check['outputs']:
                cell = output_cell(item);observation=observe_cell(cell);previous=before[output_key(item)]
                status,message=assess_linkage_output(previous,observation,item.get('expected'))
                if message:result['errors' if status=='failed' else 'pending'].append(message+':'+item.get('sheet',check['sheet'])+'!'+item['cell'])
                output_results.append({'sheet':item.get('sheet',check['sheet']),'cell':item['cell'],'status':status,'before':previous['value'],'after':observation['value'],'before_text':previous['text'],'after_text':observation['text'],'before_observation':previous,'after_observation':observation,'expected':item.get('expected'),'expected_provided':'expected' in item})
            result['linkage_checks'].append({'sheet': check['sheet'], 'input_cell': check['input_cell'], 'outputs': output_results, 'status': 'failed' if any(x['status'] == 'failed' for x in output_results) else ('pending' if any(x['status'] == 'pending' for x in output_results) else 'verified')})
            target.Value2 = check['input_expected']; app.CalculateFullRebuild()
        result['verified'] = not result['errors'] and not result['pending']
    except Exception as exc:
        result['pending'].append('原生引擎或缓存无法证实:' + str(exc))
    finally:
        if book is not None:
            try: book.Close(False)
            except Exception: pass
        if app is not None:
            for setting, value in original_settings.items():
                try: setattr(app, setting, value)
                except Exception: pass
            # WPS的DispatchEx可能复用实例，只关闭本工具打开的副本。
            # 仅实际新进程且已无其他工作簿时退出应用，不影响既有会话。
            try:
                if independent and int(app.Workbooks.Count) == 0: app.Quit()
            except Exception: pass
        if mutex is not None:
            kernel.ReleaseMutex(mutex); kernel.CloseHandle(mutex)
    return result

def verify_native_copy(path, expected_sha256, linkage_checks=None):
    path = Path(path).resolve()
    result = {'verified': False, 'errors': [], 'pending': [], 'source_sha256': expected_sha256}
    if not path.is_file() or sha256(path) != expected_sha256:
        result['errors'].append('原生复核前实际文件SHA不符'); return result
    config = {'format': path.suffix.lower().lstrip('.'), 'formulas': [], 'xls_values': {}, 'linkage_checks': linkage_checks or []}
    if config['format'] == 'xlsx':
        from openpyxl import load_workbook
        formulas = load_workbook(path, read_only=True, data_only=False); cached = load_workbook(path, read_only=True, data_only=True)
        try:
            with ZipFile(path) as package:
                for ws in formulas:
                    ws.reset_dimensions(); values = cached[ws.title]; values.reset_dimensions()
                    value_rows = {i: row for i, row in enumerate(values.values, 1)}
                    metadata = formula_cache_metadata(package.read(ws._worksheet_path.lstrip('/')))
                    for row in ws:
                        for cell in row:
                            if cell.data_type == 'f':
                                values_row = value_rows.get(cell.row, ())
                                value = values_row[cell.column-1] if cell.column <= len(values_row) else None
                                meta = metadata.get(cell.coordinate, {'cache_present': False})
                                if meta.get('empty_string_cache'): value = ''
                                config['formulas'].append({'sheet': ws.title, 'cell': cell.coordinate, 'formula': cell.value, 'cached': value, **meta})
        finally: formulas.close(); cached.close()
    elif config['format'] == 'xls':
        import xlrd
        from openpyxl.utils.cell import get_column_letter
        book = xlrd.open_workbook(path)
        config['xls_values'] = {s.name: {f'{get_column_letter(c+1)}{r+1}': s.cell_value(r, c) for r in range(s.nrows) for c in range(s.ncols)} for s in book.sheets()}
        config['xls_errors'] = {s.name:{f'{get_column_letter(c+1)}{r+1}':xlrd.error_text_from_code.get(int(s.cell_value(r,c)),'Excel错误') for r in range(s.nrows) for c in range(s.ncols) if s.cell_type(r,c)==xlrd.XL_CELL_ERROR} for s in book.sheets()}
    else:
        result['pending'].append('实际文件不是支持原生复核的xlsx/xls'); return result
    # 临时目录限定在系统临时目录中，仅删除本次创建的隔离目录。
    parent = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()).resolve()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='payroll-native-audit-', dir=parent) as temporary:
        root = Path(temporary).resolve()
        if root.parent != parent or path.is_relative_to(root): raise ValueError('隔离目录越界')
        isolated = root / ('isolated' + path.suffix); shutil.copy2(path, isolated)
        config['isolated_path'] = str(isolated)
        input_file, output_file = root / 'config.json', root / 'result.json'
        input_file.write_text(json.dumps(config, ensure_ascii=False, default=str), encoding='utf-8')
        try:
            process = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-B', str(Path(__file__).resolve()), '--private-child', str(input_file), str(output_file)], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=45)
            if process.returncode or not output_file.is_file(): result['pending'].append('隔离原生复核未完成')
            else: result.update(json.loads(output_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8')))
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired: result['pending'].append('隔离原生复核超过45秒，不能视为通过')
    result['source_sha256_after'] = sha256(path)
    if result['source_sha256_after'] != expected_sha256:
        result['errors'].append('原生复核后原文件SHA变化'); result['verified'] = False
    return result

if __name__ == '__main__':
    if len(sys.argv) != 4 or sys.argv[1] != '--private-child': raise SystemExit('此程序由crossstage_audit在隔离副本上调用')
    configuration = json.loads(Path(sys.argv[2]).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    Path(sys.argv[3]).write_text(json.dumps(native_child(configuration), ensure_ascii=False, default=str, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
`````

## skills/payroll-operations/scripts/offer_evidence.py

`````python
"""只读提取录用函正文报到日期，核对预核记录；文件名不作为日期依据。
约定报到日不等于实际入职日，不在此覆盖花名册或裁定两者冲突。
"""
import argparse,hashlib,json,re,unicodedata
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

LABEL=re.compile(r'入职报到日期|入职日期|报到日期|报到时间')
DATE=re.compile(r'(?P<y>\d{4})\s*(?:年|[-/])\s*(?P<m>\d{1,2})\s*(?:月|[-/])\s*(?P<d>\d{1,2})\s*日?')
def normalize(text):return re.sub(r'\s+','',unicodedata.normalize('NFKC',text or ''))
def parse_pages(pages,employee=None):
    candidates=[];problems=[]
    identity=bool(employee and any(normalize(employee) in normalize(text) for text in pages))
    for page,text in enumerate(pages,1):
        compact=normalize(text)
        for label in LABEL.finditer(compact):
            tail=compact[label.end():label.end()+80]
            # 日期必须紧接字段，不能跳过其他条款去抓签订/打印日期。
            found=DATE.match(re.sub(r'^[：:，,\-—]+','',tail))
            if not found:continue
            try:value=date(int(found['y']),int(found['m']),int(found['d'])).isoformat()
            except ValueError:
                problems.append({'page':page,'label':label.group(),'reason':'正文日期不合法','quote':compact[label.start():label.end()+len(found.group())+2]});continue
            raw_quote=None
            lines=text.splitlines()
            for n,line in enumerate(lines):
                if label.group() in normalize(line):
                    excerpt=[]
                    for following in lines[n:n+4]:
                        excerpt.append(following)
                        if DATE.search(unicodedata.normalize('NFKC','\n'.join(excerpt))):break
                    raw_quote='\n'.join(excerpt);break
            candidates.append({'page':page,'label':label.group(),'reporting_date':value,'quote':compact[label.start():label.end()+len(found.group())+2],'raw_quote':raw_quote})
    dates=sorted({x['reporting_date'] for x in candidates})
    if not identity:problems.append({'reason':'正文未能核实本人员姓名；不能仅用文件名确认归属'})
    if not candidates:problems.append({'reason':'未取得可核验的正文报到日期；扫描件或特殊格式待人工提取，不回退文件名'})
    elif len(dates)!=1:problems.append({'reason':'正文存在多个不同报到日期，待人工核定'})
    return {'status':'pending' if problems else 'extracted','employee':employee,'reporting_date':dates[0] if len(dates)==1 and not problems else None,'candidates':candidates,'pending':problems,'note':'仅核约定报到日正文事实，不证明实际入职日、薪资标准或人工确认'}
def inspect(pdf,employee,expected_sha256,reported_date=None):
    from pypdf import PdfReader
    pdf=Path(pdf).resolve();digest=hashlib.sha256(pdf.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    if digest!=expected_sha256:raise ValueError('录用函SHA与事先固定原件不一致')
    pages=[p.extract_text() or '' for p in PdfReader(pdf).pages]
    result=parse_pages(pages,employee);result.update(schema='payroll-offer-evidence/1',source={'path':str(pdf),'sha256':digest},reported_date=reported_date,errors=[])
    if reported_date is not None and result['reporting_date'] is not None and reported_date!=result['reporting_date']:
        result['errors'].append('预核记录日期与正文日期不一致；不得采用文件名日期或虚构冲突')
    result['passed']=not result['errors'] and result['status']=='extracted'
    return result
def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--pdf',type=Path,required=True);p.add_argument('--employee',required=True);p.add_argument('--expected-sha256',required=True);p.add_argument('--reported-date');p.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True)
    a=p.parse_args()
    if a.output.exists() or a.output.resolve()==a.pdf.resolve():raise SystemExit('证据必须另存，不能覆盖录用函或既有结果')
    try:r=inspect(a.pdf,a.employee,a.expected_sha256,a.reported_date)
    except Exception as exc:r={'passed':False,'errors':[str(exc)],'status':'pending'}
    a.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);a.output.write_text(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8');print(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False));raise SystemExit(0 if r['passed'] else 2)
if __name__=='__main__':main()
`````

## skills/payroll-operations/scripts/payroll_comment_review.py

`````python
"""原工资批注的业务处置复核；对象复制不等于清算事项已处理。"""
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import re
from decimal import Decimal,InvalidOperation
from openpyxl import load_workbook

ACTIONS={'adopted','explanation','historical_not_applicable','pending'}

def settlement_comment(text):
    return bool(re.search(r'事假[\s\S]*(?:清算|结算|結算|结清|結清)|(?:清算|结算|結算|结清|結清)[\s\S]*事假',text))

def review_comments(inventory, people, month, dispositions, resolve_ref, resolve_components=None):
    """inventory必须来自实际template_guard.verify；resolve_ref返回实际值/行/源。"""
    rows=[]
    for group in (inventory or {}).get('sources',[]):
        source=group['source'];path=Path(source['path'])
        if hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=source['sha256']:raise ValueError('业务批注源SHA已变化')
        book=load_workbook(path,data_only=False)
        try:
            for item in group.get('items',[]):
                original=book[item['sheet']][item['cell']].comment
                if original is None or original.text!=item['text']:raise ValueError('实际原批注与盘点不符')
                text=original.text;identities=item.get('identity') or []
                owners=[key for key,p in people.items() if p['name'] in identities]
                risky=settlement_comment(text) and item.get('status')!='ignored_historical_sheet'
                row={'source_path':str(path),'source_sha256':source['sha256'],'source_sheet':item['sheet'],'source_cell':item['cell'],'original_text':text,'object_status':item.get('status'),'employee_key':owners[0] if len(owners)==1 else None,'name':people[owners[0]]['name'] if len(owners)==1 else ';'.join(identities),'requires_business_resolution':risky,'action':'explanation','effective_status':'resolved','reason':'普通说明；未识别到本门槛限定的事假清算事项','evidence_refs':[]}
                rows.append(row)
                if not risky:continue
                row.update(action='pending',effective_status='pending',reason='原批注涉及事假清算，尚无实际本人本月处置依据')
                if len(owners)!=1:row['reason']='清算批注不能唯一定位本次人员，需核人员去向';continue
                key=owners[0]
                actual_names=[str(c.value or '').strip() for c in book[item['sheet']][item['row']]]
                if people[key]['name'] not in actual_names:raise ValueError('原批注实际行身份与盘点不符')
                choices=[d for d in dispositions if d.get('employee_key')==key and d.get('source_sha256')==source['sha256'] and d.get('source_sheet')==item['sheet'] and d.get('source_cell')==item['cell']]
                if len(choices)!=1:continue
                decision=choices[0];action=decision.get('action')
                if action not in ACTIONS or not decision.get('reason'):row['reason']='逐条处置缺明确采用/仅说明/历史不适用/待确认及理由';continue
                row.update(action=action,reason=decision['reason'])
                if action=='pending':continue
                refs=decision.get('evidence_refs',[])
                try:
                    if not refs:raise ValueError('缺既有实际处置来源')
                    texts=[]
                    for ref in refs:
                        value,actual,origin=resolve_ref(ref,key)
                        if origin.get('month')!=month or actual.get('month')!=month:raise ValueError('处置证据缺实际本月归属或属于其他月份')
                        texts.append(str(value))
                    evidence='\n'.join(texts)
                    if not settlement_comment(evidence):raise ValueError('本人来源未定位事假清算事项；全员泛称未确认不能代替')
                    if any(t in evidence for t in ('待确认','待確認','未确认','未確認','待核','尚未')):raise ValueError('实际来源仍为未决，不能自报已处置')
                    tokens={'adopted':('已计入','已計入','本月采用','本月採用','本月结算','本月結算'),'explanation':('仅说明','僅說明','仅备注','僅備註','不影响本月','不影響本月'),'historical_not_applicable':('已结算','已結算','已结清','已結清','历史不适用','歷史不適用','不重复','不重複')}
                    if not any(t in evidence for t in tokens[action]):raise ValueError('实际处置文字不支持所选处理')
                    if action=='adopted':
                        ref=decision.get('amount_ref')
                        if not ref:raise ValueError('采用清算须指向本人已核扣款金额，程序不代算')
                        if not any((r.get('table'),r.get('row'))==(ref.get('table'),ref.get('row')) for r in refs):raise ValueError('采用金额须来自同一本人处置记录行，不能引用无关数字')
                        value,actual,origin=resolve_ref(ref,key)
                        if origin.get('month')!=month or actual.get('month')!=month or isinstance(value,bool) or not isinstance(value,(int,float)):raise ValueError('采用金额不是本人本月已核数字')
                        row['adopted_amount']=value;row['amount_ref']=ref
                        scope=decision.get('amount_scope','component')
                        if scope=='total_leave':
                            if not any(t in evidence for t in ('完整请假扣款','完整請假扣款','请假扣款总额','請假扣款總額','S总额','S總額')):raise ValueError('原处置未明确核定完整S总额，不能把清算子项当完整请假扣款')
                            row['adopted_total_leave']=value
                        elif scope=='component':
                            components=decision.get('component_refs',[]);total_ref=decision.get('total_leave_ref')
                            if not components or not total_ref or resolve_components is None:raise ValueError('清算仅为子项，缺完整请假扣款组成及总额依据，继续待核')
                            locations=[(r.get('table'),r.get('row'),r.get('column')) for r in components]
                            if len(locations)!=len(set(locations)):raise ValueError('清算组成引用重复，不能重复计入')
                            if (ref.get('table'),ref.get('row'),ref.get('column')) not in locations:raise ValueError('完整组成未包含本人已核清算子项')
                            tables={r.get('table') for r in components}
                            if len(tables)!=1:raise ValueError('完整组成须引用同一实际明细表，以核全项覆盖')
                            expected=resolve_components(next(iter(tables)),key)
                            if set(locations)!={(r['table'],r['row'],r['column']) for r in expected}:raise ValueError('完整请假扣款组成遗漏或越出实际同人本月明细')
                            subtotal=Decimal(0);ids=set()
                            for part in components:
                                amount,actual,origin=resolve_ref(part,key)
                                component_id=actual.get('component_id')
                                if not component_id or component_id in ids:raise ValueError('实际明细项目缺唯一标识或重复，不能重复计入清算')
                                ids.add(component_id)
                                if origin.get('month')!=month or actual.get('month')!=month or isinstance(amount,bool) or not isinstance(amount,(int,float)):raise ValueError('完整组成仍有非本人本月已核数字')
                                subtotal+=Decimal(str(amount))
                            total,actual,origin=resolve_ref(total_ref,key)
                            if total_ref.get('column')!='total_leave' or origin.get('month')!=month or actual.get('month')!=month or isinstance(total,bool) or not isinstance(total,(int,float)):raise ValueError('缺实际本人本月完整请假扣款总额定位')
                            if not subtotal.is_finite() or not Decimal(str(total)).is_finite() or subtotal!=Decimal(str(total)):raise ValueError('实际完整组成合计与已核完整请假扣款总额不符')
                            row.update(adopted_total_leave=total,component_refs=components,total_leave_ref=total_ref,component_sum=str(subtotal))
                        else:raise ValueError('清算金额范围须为component或明确total_leave')
                    row.update(effective_status='resolved',evidence_refs=refs)
                except (ValueError,KeyError,TypeError,InvalidOperation) as exc:row.update(effective_status='pending',reason=str(exc))
        finally:book.close()
        if hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=source['sha256']:raise ValueError('读取业务批注期间源SHA变化')
    return rows
`````

## skills/payroll-operations/scripts/payroll_core.py

`````python
"""Confirmed-input payroll arithmetic. No salary guessing, tax estimation or brokerage writes.
Amounts use Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP at final item; policy ambiguities return pending.
"""
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP, InvalidOperation
from datetime import date
import calendar

def number(value):
    if value is None or str(value).strip()=='':raise ValueError('缺少已确认数字')
    try:value=Decimal(str(value))
    except InvalidOperation as exc:raise ValueError('已确认数字无法解析') from exc
    if not value.is_finite():raise ValueError('数字必须有限')
    return value
def money(value):return float(number(value).quantize(Decimal('.01'),rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP))
def personal_leave_deduction(salary,hours,intern,required_hours):
    denominator=number(required_hours) if intern else Decimal(174)
    if denominator<=0 or number(hours)<0:raise ValueError('工时分母须大于0；负结余另核')
    return money(-number(salary)/denominator*number(hours))
def maternity_difference(normal,maternity,workdays,maternity_days):
    if number(workdays)<=0 or not 0<=number(maternity_days)<=number(workdays):raise ValueError('产假工作日范围错误')
    return money((number(normal)-number(maternity))/number(workdays)*number(maternity_days))
def increment_amount(initial,regular,probation_months):
    if probation_months==3:return 0
    if probation_months!=6:raise ValueError('未定义的试用期递增口径')
    return money((number(regular)-number(initial))/5)
def cycle_boundary(hire,months):
    y,m=divmod(hire.year*12+hire.month-1+months,12);m+=1
    day=min(hire.day,calendar.monthrange(y,m)[1])
    if day!=hire.day:raise ValueError('周期对应日不存在，须确认月底周期边界')
    return date(y,m,day)
def prorate(amount,total_days,applicable_days):
    if number(total_days)<=0 or not 0<=number(applicable_days)<=number(total_days):raise ValueError('计薪天数范围错误')
    return money(number(amount)/number(total_days)*number(applicable_days))
def hire_exit_difference(salary,paid_days,unpaid_days):return -prorate(salary,paid_days,unpaid_days)
def sick_leave_deduction(salary,hours,required_hours,prior_hours,minimum_wage=None):
    try:
        h,prior,den=number(hours),number(prior_hours),number(required_hours)
        if den<=0 or min(h,prior)<0:raise ValueError('病假工时无效')
        if prior<72<prior+h:return dict(status='pending',amount=None,reason='本月跨年度9天阈值，分段制度尚未确认')
        if prior+h<=72:amount=-number(salary)/den*h/2
        else:amount=-(number(salary)-number(minimum_wage)*Decimal('.8'))/den*h
        return dict(status='calculated',amount=money(amount),reason='按已确认病假档位；最低工资须使用当月已核准来源')
`````
